# House of Hypertrophy - master bibliography

Every source cited across the notes in this folder, deduplicated by PubMed ID and DOI.

**745 resolved papers** and **187 links we could not resolve**, across 143 videos that cite anything.

Sorted so that papers cited by several videos come first - those are the ones doing the most work.

---
